Two ways a real room silently drops out of floor, tile, plaster and waterproofing today: (1) `arch_rooms.py:54` MIN_SPACE_M2 = 1.0 makes any face under 1.0 m2 a SLIVER with no room, so a labelled small W.C., shower or duct vanishes; (2) an unlabelled room with no fixtures is typed with no code and takes neither wet nor dry scope (T-019 finding 3, SV-C).
